# Catalogo de itens do Steambirds Alliance

Gerado pelo servidor a partir do configBinary da build **201219.1413** (1593 itens). Nome e texto de lore em ingles, como no jogo.

Para cada item: id, nome, tier, quem pode usar (classes pelo `tag` do item e `allowedItemTags` da classe; sem tag = todas as classes), stats e o texto de descricao/lore. Valor de reciclagem pela config `mulching`.

## Resumo

| Tipo | Itens |
|---|---|
| Arma principal (`MainGun`) | 389 |
| Ataque secundario (`SecondaryAttack`) | 169 |
| Motor (`Engine`) | 72 |
| Armadura (`Armor`) | 76 |
| Melhoria permanente (`Upgrade`) | 17 |
| Melhoria permanente (usuario) (`UserUpgrade`) | 6 |
| Vida (coleta) (`Health`) | 12 |
| Vapor (coleta) (`Energy`) | 13 |
| Consumivel (`ConsumableAction`) | 690 |
| Missao / material (`Quest`) | 106 |
| Portal / chave (`Portal`) | 41 |
| Arma de morte (`DeathGun`) | 1 |
| Arma de manobra (`ManeuverGun`) | 1 |

### `healaura_1` — Heal Aura (tier 1)
> Gradually heals allies nearby. 'Auras. We treat these as the most mundane of technologies. The micro-manufacturing, the energy field generators; all of these various species have used for hundreds of years. Yet even our most advanced coggers could not fully describe why they work.'
- **Pode usar:** Paladin | **Tag:** `healaura` | **Reciclagem:** 1 newscrap
- nuvem healaura1 (healAura, efeito 1.7, raio 5, dura 10s, custo 70 vapor, recarga 13s)

### `healaura_2` — Mercy Aura (tier 2)
> Gradually heals allies nearby. 'One theory states we live in an extra-dimensional timescape. Where an objects exists in a continuum of states, both destroyed and not destroyed. The aura then forces our reality to remember a time before it was damaged. Pragmatists scoff; all that matters is it works.'
- **Pode usar:** Paladin | **Tag:** `healaura` | **Reciclagem:** 5 newscrap
- nuvem healaura2 (healAura, efeito 1.9, raio 5, dura 10s, custo 70 vapor, recarga 13s)

### `healaura_3` — Super Heal Aura (tier 3)
> Gradually heals allies nearby. 'Technology exists in layers of history. After the Intervention, we discovered language, sticks, levers and wheels. Then cogs, orbitals and steam. And chemistry, atomics, hotboxes. But with each new discovery, we still use the old inventions. Mixed up, mashed up, augmented. Thousands of years of mundane innovation form the essential unspoken foundation that civilization rests upon.'
- **Pode usar:** Paladin | **Tag:** `healaura` | **Reciclagem:** 10 newscrap
- nuvem healaura3 (healAura, efeito 2.2, raio 5, dura 10s, custo 70 vapor, recarga 13s)

### `healaura_4` — Mega Heal Aura (tier 4)
> Gradually heals allies nearby. 'There was a period after the old gyptians when auras were a lost tech. Grubbers would find working examples in various 'miraculous' artifacts. The Amber Rib and its healing wounds. The Crying Creche. But attempts to independently replicate the repair process always failed.'
- **Pode usar:** Paladin | **Tag:** `healaura` | **Reciclagem:** 25 newscrap
- nuvem healaura4 (healAura, efeito 2.6, raio 5, dura 10s, custo 70 vapor, recarga 13s)

### `healaura_5` — Repair Aura (tier 5)
> Auras didn't return to production status until the advent of workable fusion combined with the well-understood reliability of steam engines. The underlying techniques required an explosive persistence of mechanical energy (see Eival-Dogson Work Function, 1202) that simply did not exist in the natural world.
- **Pode usar:** Paladin | **Tag:** `healaura` | **Reciclagem:** 50 newscrap
- nuvem healaura5 (healAura, efeito 2.8, raio 5, dura 10s, custo 70 vapor, recarga 13s)

### `turret_1` — Basic Turret (tier 1)

### `turret_2` — Plague Turret (tier 2)
> Spawns a stationary gun. Basic bio-engineering gives us substantial protection against most natural illnesses. However, it does nothing to protect us from phages left hiding in ancient technologies. These mechanical plagues cleanse whole territories with disturbing regularity.
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 5 newscrap
- invoca allyturret2 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_3` — Assault Turret (tier 3)
> Spawns a stationary gun. The Endless Plague incubated in a population of tunneling scavenger rodents, uplifted from a highly prolific Microtinae subfamily. The initial infection vector was rumored to be a black floating cube recovered from the body cavity of a buried pregyptian colossus. Details are, of course, lost.
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 10 newscrap
- invoca allyturret3 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_4` — Eiffel Turret (tier 4)
> Spawns a stationary gun. The earliest field reports (Culmette Files, circa 1507) note the abnormally increased reproduction. Matter, particularly mammalian flesh was suctioned up through hollow serrated hairs of an infected subject. And rapidly reconstituted into virulent babies.
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 25 newscrap
- invoca allyturret4 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_5` — Bastille Turret (tier 5)
> The infection spread quietly through the burrows. A temperature change, the first warm day of spring, seems to have triggered the lemming horde's eruption to surface. At this point, in the recovered writings of journalist Gullston Calmette, they were 'a vast churning sea of hunger'
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 50 newscrap
- invoca allyturret5 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_6` — Blasted Turret (tier 6)
> The initial wave engulfed much of the porcine quarter. The Red Wall, famous for the thousands of bouquets left each April at its base, commemorates their passing. The sus-ungulate line has sadly never fully recovered and we sing ballads of their lonely decline.
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 200 newscrap
- invoca allyturret6 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_7` — Ruined Turret (tier 7)

- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 400 newscrap
- invoca allyturret7 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_8` — Haunted Pillbox (tier 8)
> The lemmings repeatedly attacked a west-side embankment manned by a single 75-year old factory cogger, the mythic Henri Spiner. For 134 days, endless attacks were turned aside by a lone pillbox. When rescue finally arrived, they found Spiner long dead of starvation. In his final days, he'd perfected an automated turret and hooked it to an eternal hotbox-based steam generator.
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Reciclagem:** 800 newscrap
- invoca allyturret8 x1 (recarga 1s)

### `turret_9` — Midnight Turret (tier 9)
> Hand-copied blueprints of the Spiner-turret enabled pockets of survivors to cobble together their own automated defense systems. Slowly a network of defended bunkers and tunnels spread across the ruined city. Children of the time say their fondest memories were of the soothing thunk, thunk, thunk as guns chewed through that endless sea of screaming lemmings.
- **Pode usar:** Engineer | **Tag:** `turret` | **Marcas:** soulbound, pode ser supercarregado | **Reciclagem:** 2000 newscrap

## Missao / material (`Quest`)

### `DQ_CS_11LOW_deer_T3_moose_meat_horde` — Moose Meat (tier -1)
> A large pile of bioengineered moose meat.
- **Marcas:** nao negociavel

### `DQ_CS_12LOW_ninjaA_T2_bomb_cat_bomb` — Bomb Cat Bomb (tier -1)
> Each Bomb Cat Bomb is assigned to a cat.  The cat that the Bomb Cat Bomb is assigned to is called the 'Bomb Cat Bomb Cat.'
- **Marcas:** nao negociavel

### `DQ_CS_13LOW_willowisp_T2_toxic_crystal` — Gas Canister (tier -1)
> Poisonous gas, condensed and frozen. Enough to sterilize 10 square kilometers.
- **Marcas:** nao negociavel

### `DQ_CS_21MID_minelayer_dog_waste` — Dog Manure (tier -1)
> It smells...awful. But keeps our Victory gardens producing beautiful tomatoes.
- **Marcas:** nao negociavel

### `DQ_CS_22MID_wraith_T3_ashes` — Ashes (tier -1)
> Said to contain the souls of downed pilots.
- **Marcas:** nao negociavel

### `DQ_CS_23MID_crawler_fisher_crab_parts` — Fisher Crab Parts (tier -1)
> The remains of a destroyed Fisher Crab.  Must have been old...it's covered in barnacles.
- **Marcas:** nao negociavel

### `DQ_CS_31HGH_angel2_bio_interface_prototype` — Bio-Interface Prototype (tier -1)
> Torture devices that are strapped on to the Empire's slave pilots to make sure they follow orders.
